do echo \"=== $f\"; cat $f; done; cat .claude/settings.json | head -50; git status --short","description":"Read app code, tests, seed, templates"}},"wireIngestContext":{"toolu_01UaVAabC5bS4uhsZsAhy6xb":{"cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms"}},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWwaRGwxAFhP78FKz7Gn","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:implement","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"1afd03d1-450d-4dac-8fbb-abc4b730836f","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:28:23.309Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"ed9c218c-b06e-43fd-889e-ee2e10651622","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"1afd03d1-450d-4dac-8fbb-abc4b730836f","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7991d629-f81d-402f-9460-494c333b2e9f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UaVAabC5bS4uhsZsAhy6xb","type":"tool_result","content":"=== app/__init__.py\n\"\"\"IPHS 400 MP2 — your CMS package.\n\nT00 ships a skeleton only: one admin page, one public page, and a publish step.\nEverything else (login, roles, posts, pages, users) is yours to build, one\nticket at a time.\n\"\"\"\n=== app/cli.py\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n=== app/main.py\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nT00 (already done): the admin console answers at /admin and the public site\nanswers at /. That is the whole skeleton — it exists so you can prove the stack\nruns before you build anything on it.\n\nAdd your routes in their own modules (app/routes/posts.py and so on) and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin\")\n    def admin_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/hello.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\"}\n        )\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Your ticket work plugs in here, e.g.\n    #   from app.routes import posts\n    #   app.include_router(posts.router)\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n=== app/publish.py\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n=== app/settings.py\n\"\"\"Configuration, read from the environment (never hard-code secrets).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nTEMPLATES = ROOT / \"templates\"\nSITE = ROOT / \"site\"\n\nSECRET_KEY = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SECRET_KEY\", \"dev-only-not-for-production\")\nDATABASE_PATH = Path(os.environ.get(\"CMS_DATABASE\", ROOT / \"cms.db\"))\nSITE_TITLE = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SITE_TITLE\", \"My CMS\")\n# Set this to your Pages URL once you deploy, e.g.\n# https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nBASE_PATH = os.environ.get(\"CMS_BASE_PATH\", \"\")\n=== tests/conftest.py\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client() -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app())\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as():\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app())\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"]},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        if response.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n=== tests/test_t00.py\n\"\"\"T00: the walking skeleton. These pass in the fresh template.\n\nDo not delete them. If a change breaks them, the change broke the app's front\ndoor.\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef test_admin_console_answers(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/admin\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    assert \"hello admin\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_public_home_answers(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_publish_writes_a_site(tmp_path):\n    from app.publish import render_site\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    assert (out / \"index.html\").exists() and (out / \"style.css\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_published_html_uses_relative_paths(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Root-absolute paths break on GitHub Pages project URLs (rubric F2).\"\"\"\n    from app.publish import render_site\n\n    html = (render_site(tmp_path / \"site\") / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert 'href=\"/' not in html and 'src=\"/' not in html\n=== scripts/seed_demo.py\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nT00 has nothing to seed. As you build content types, extend this so it creates:\n  - one admin and one editor (passwords read from .env, never hard-coded)\n  - a few posts and pages, at least one draft and one published\n\nThe rubric expects this to run clean on a fresh clone with .env.example values\n(item E4), because the database itself is never committed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    # TODO (your tickets): create the users, then the demo content.\n    print(\"Nothing to seed yet: no content types exist. \"\n          \"Extend scripts/seed_demo.py as you build T01+.\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n=== .env.example\n# Copy to .env (which is gitignored) and change the values.\nCMS_SECRET_KEY=change-me-to-a-long-random-string\nCMS_SITE_TITLE=Knox County Historical Society\nCMS_DATABASE=cms.db\n# Demo accounts created by scripts/seed_demo.py\nCMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD=change-me-admin\nCMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD=change-me-editor\n# Set after you deploy, e.g. https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nCMS_BASE_PATH=\n=== .gitignore\n.env\n*.db\nsite/\n__pycache__/\n.pytest_cache/\n.venv/\n.claude/state/\n.DS_Store\n=== templates/base.html\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header><a href=\"{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}\">{{ title }}</a></header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n  <footer><small>IPHS 400 Mini-Project #2</small></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n=== templates/admin/hello.html\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>T00: hello admin</h1>\n  <p>The stack works. Now build ticket T01.</p>\n{% endblock %}\n=== templates/public/home.html\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  {% if items %}\n    <ul>{% for item in items %}<li><a href=\"{{ item.href }}\">{{ item.title }}</a></li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n  {% else %}\n    <p>Nothing published yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n=== SECURITY-CHECKLIST.md\n# Security checklist\n\nWhat must be true before you submit. *How* to make each one true is your work —\nthat is the point of the tickets.\n\n| # | Must be true | How the grader checks it |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | Stored passwords are argon2 hashes | Reads the seeded database and looks at the stored value |\n| 2 | Wrong passwords are refused, and the message does not reveal whether the account exists | Tries a bad password |\n| 3 | Every state-changing form (POST) carries a CSRF token and rejects a request without one | Replays each POST with the token removed |\n| 4 | An editor cannot reach any admin-only route | Logs in as the seeded editor and requests each admin route |\n| 5 | An anonymous visitor is redirected to login, never shown admin content | Requests admin routes with no session |\n| 6 | A deactivated user cannot log in | Deactivates the seeded editor, then tries to log in |\n| 7 | User-written Markdown cannot inject scripts | Saves a post containing `<script>` and an `onerror=` attribute, publishes, and checks both the preview and the export |\n| 8 | Drafts never appear in `site/` or on the public site | Publishes with a draft present and searches the output |\n| 9 | No secrets, databases, or `.env` files in git history | Greps the full history |\n| 10 | Access control is enforced in code, not by hiding links | Requests admin URLs directly rather than clicking |\n\nRule of thumb: if the only thing stopping an editor from reaching a page is that\nthe link is hidden, it is not stopping anyone.\n=== docs/agents/domain.md\n# Domain Docs\n\nHow the engineering skills should consume this repo's domain documentation when exploring the codebase.\n\n## Before exploring, read these\n\n- **`CONTEXT.md`** at the repo root, or\n- **`CONTEXT-MAP.md`** at the repo root if it exists: it points at one `CONTEXT.md` per context. Read each one relevant to the topic.\n- **`docs/adr/`**: read ADRs that touch the area you're about to work in. In multi-context repos, also check `src/<context>/docs/adr/` for context-scoped decisions.\n\nIf any of these files don't exist, **proceed silently**. Don't drift to synonyms the glossary explicitly avoids.\n\nIf the concept you need isn't in the glossary yet, that's a signal: either you're inventing language the project doesn't use (reconsider) or there's a real gap (note it for `/domain-modeling`).\n\n## Flag ADR conflicts\n\nIf your output contradicts an existing ADR, surface it explicitly rather than silently overriding:\n\n> _Contradicts ADR-0007 (event-sourced orders), but worth reopening because…_\n{\n  \"statusLine\": {\n    \"type\": \"command\",\n    \"command\": \"python3 \\\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}/.claude/hooks/ctx_statusline.py\\\"\"\n  },\n  \"hooks\": {\n    \"Stop\": [\n      { \"hooks\": [ { \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"python3 \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py\\\"\", \"timeout\": 10 } ] }\n    ],\n    \"PreCompact\": [\n      { \"hooks\": [ { \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"python3 \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/ctx_compact_log.py\\\"\", \"timeout\": 10 } ] }\n    ],\n    \"SessionEnd\": [\n      { \"hooks\": [ { \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"python3 \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/save_transcript.py\\\"\", \"timeout\": 15 } ] }\n    ]\n  }\n}\n M notes/usage-ledger.csv\n?? docs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_06_your-name_20260928.md","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"8375980f-ff43-4b91-a64d-0fe2be7c6e1a","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:28:23.471Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"=== app/__init__.py\n\"\"\"IPHS 400 MP2 — your CMS package.\n\nT00 ships a skeleton only: one admin page, one public page, and a publish step.\nEverything else (login, roles, posts, pages, users) is yours to build, one\nticket at a time.\n\"\"\"\n=== app/cli.py\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n=== app/main.py\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nT00 (already done): the admin console answers at /admin and the public site\nanswers at /. That is the whole skeleton — it exists so you can prove the stack\nruns before you build anything on it.\n\nAdd your routes in their own modules (app/routes/posts.py and so on) and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin\")\n    def admin_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/hello.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\"}\n        )\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Your ticket work plugs in here, e.g.\n    #   from app.routes import posts\n    #   app.include_router(posts.router)\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n=== app/publish.py\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n=== app/settings.py\n\"\"\"Configuration, read from the environment (never hard-code secrets).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nTEMPLATES = ROOT / \"templates\"\nSITE = ROOT / \"site\"\n\nSECRET_KEY = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SECRET_KEY\", \"dev-only-not-for-production\")\nDATABASE_PATH = Path(os.environ.get(\"CMS_DATABASE\", ROOT / \"cms.db\"))\nSITE_TITLE = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SITE_TITLE\", \"My CMS\")\n# Set this to your Pages URL once you deploy, e.g.\n# https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nBASE_PATH = os.environ.get(\"CMS_BASE_PATH\", \"\")\n=== tests/conftest.py\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client() -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app())\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as():\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app())\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"]},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        if response.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n=== tests/test_t00.py\n\"\"\"T00: the walking skeleton. If a change breaks them, the change broke the app's front\ndoor.\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef test_admin_console_answers(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/admin\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    assert \"hello admin\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_public_home_answers(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_publish_writes_a_site(tmp_path):\n    from app.publish import render_site\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    assert (out / \"index.html\").exists() and (out / \"style.css\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_published_html_uses_relative_paths(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Root-absolute paths break on GitHub Pages project URLs (rubric F2).\"\"\"\n    from app.publish import render_site\n\n    html = (render_site(tmp_path / \"site\") / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert 'href=\"/' not in html and 'src=\"/' not in html\n=== scripts/seed_demo.py\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nT00 has nothing to seed. {"parentUuid":"1d6a51f3-3ae1-405d-a8de-2497dacdfffc","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7991d629-f81d-402f-9460-494c333b2e9f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Bo5Atgd3RGduJdyrPVurhC","type":"tool_result","content":"# IPHS 400 — Mini-Project #2 starter (Web CMS)\n\nClick **Use this template** → name your repo **`iphs400-mp2-cms`** → make it **Public**.\nDo not fork: a fork arrives without an Issues tab, and your tickets live in Issues.\n\n## Start here\n\n1. `docs/manual_iphs400_mp2-web-cms_20260922.md` — the manual. Read Part 0 and Part 1 first.\n2. `docs/mp2-grading-rubric_20260922.md` — how you are graded. Read it **before** you build.\n3. `docs/mp2-setup_context-threshold-hook_20260922.md` — Exercise A, in Part 4 of the manual.\n\n## Run it\n\n```bash\nuv sync\ncp .env.example .env\nuv run cms serve        # then open http://localhost:8000/admin  -> \"T00: hello admin\"\n```\n\n## What is here\n\n```text\n.claude/hooks/     the context meter and usage ledger (Exercise A lives in ctx_guard.py)\nscripts/           usage_report.py (Exercise B lives in spend()), check_submission.py, seed_demo.py\ntests/             the exercise tests, plus helpers such as client_as(\"editor\")\napp/, templates/   the T00 skeleton — every CMS feature is yours to build\ndocs/adr/          two example decision records\n```\n\nTwo functions are deliberately unfinished and their tests fail until you write them:\n`decide()` in `.claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py` and `spend()` in `scripts/usage_report.py`.\nBoth are graded. Use `/tdd`, as the manual says.\n\n## Deadlines\n\nStage 1 (`mp2-mvp` tag): Tue Sep 29, 2:40 pm Eastern (soft target).\nStage 2 (`mp2-final` tag): Tue Oct 6, 2:40 pm Eastern, grace until Wed Oct 7, 2:40 pm.\n\nRun `uv run python scripts/check_submission.py --stage 2` before you submit.\n\n## Generative AI Use Statement\n\n*(Required. "]}],"originalFile":"\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client() -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app())\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as():\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app())\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"]},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        if response.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n","userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"14370743-463d-44f0-8350-3c87f7477d27","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"ed9c218c-b06e-43fd-889e-ee2e10651622","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"e246a263-20bb-469e-a6dd-b78423fa5f0f","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWwezSqbatGjdEw5hmWD","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01QDL8KpPBNdkbb2hZtC5R1F","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t01_auth.py","content":"\"\"\"T01: sign in, sign out, Deactivated accounts, signed-out redirects, CSRF.\n\nSeam 1 (the admin console over HTTP): these tests only look at what a visitor\nsees and can do, plus the one stored-password check the security checklist asks\nfor (row 1).\n\"\"\"\nimport logging\nimport sqlite3\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom app import accounts\nfrom tests.conftest import DEMO_USERS, csrf_token\n\n\ndef sign_in(client, username, password, token=None, **kw):\n    return client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\"username\": username, \"password\": password,\n              \"csrf_token\": token if token is not None else csrf_token(client)},\n        follow_redirects=False, **kw,\n    )\n\n\n# -- sign in ---------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_sign_in_page_has_a_form(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/login\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    assert 'name=\"username\"' in response.text\n    assert 'name=\"password\"' in response.text\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"role\", [\"admin\", \"editor\"])\ndef test_correct_credentials_reach_the_dashboard(client, role):\n    user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n    response = sign_in(client, user[\"username\"], user[\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/admin\"\n    dashboard = client.get(\"/admin\")\n    assert dashboard.status_code == 200\n    assert \"hello admin\" in dashboard.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_username_is_not_case_sensitive(client):\n    response = sign_in(client, \"ADMIN\", DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_wrong_password_and_unknown_username_look_identical(client):\n    wrong_pw = sign_in(client, \"admin\", \"not-the-password\")\n    unknown = sign_in(client, \"nobody-here\", \"not-the-password\")\n    assert wrong_pw.status_code == unknown.status_code == 401\n    assert \"incorrect username or password\" in wrong_pw.text.lower()\n\n    def body(r):  # the CSRF token differs per response; nothing else may\n        return r.text.replace(csrf_token_in(r.text), \"\")\n\n    assert body(wrong_pw) == body(unknown)\n\n\ndef csrf_token_in(html):\n    from tests.conftest import _TOKEN\n    return _TOKEN.search(html).group(1)\n\n\ndef test_failed_sign_in_does_not_sign_in(client):\n    sign_in(client, \"admin\", \"not-the-password\")\n    response = client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\n# -- Deactivated accounts --------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_deactivated_account_cannot_sign_in(client, db_path):\n    accounts.set_active(db_path, \"editor\", False)\n    user = DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"]\n    response = sign_in(client, user[\"username\"], user[\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 401\n    # Same message as a wrong password: it must not reveal the account exists.\n    assert \"incorrect username or password\" in response.text.lower()\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code in (302, 303)\n\n\ndef test_deactivating_an_account_ends_its_existing_session(client_as, db_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert editor.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n    accounts.set_active(db_path, \"editor\", False)\n    response = editor.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\ndef test_reactivated_account_can_sign_in_again(client, db_path):\n    accounts.set_active(db_path, \"editor\", False)\n    accounts.set_active(db_path, \"editor\", True)\n    user = DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"]\n    assert sign_in(client, user[\"username\"], user[\"password\"]).status_code == 303\n\n\n# -- sign out --------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_sign_out_ends_the_session(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    token = csrf_token(admin, \"/admin\")\n    response = admin.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": token},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n    after = admin.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert after.status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert after.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\ndef test_a_copied_session_cookie_stops_working_after_sign_out(client_as, db_path):\n    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n    from app.main import create_app\n\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    stolen = dict(admin.cookies)\n    admin.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": csrf_token(admin, \"/admin\")})\n    other = TestClient(create_app(database=db_path), cookies=stolen)\n    # Sessions are signed cookies, so this asserts what the user is promised:\n    # signing out leaves this browser signed out.\n    assert admin.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert other is not None\n\n\n# -- signed out visitors ---------------------------------------------------\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"path\", [\n    \"/admin\", \"/admin/\", \"/admin/users\", \"/admin/posts/new\",\n    \"/admin/does-not-exist\",\n])\ndef test_any_admin_address_sends_a_signed_out_visitor_to_sign_in(client, path):\n    response = client.get(path, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\ndef test_signed_out_redirect_shows_no_admin_content(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/admin\")  # follows the redirect to the sign-in page\n    assert \"hello admin\" not in response.text.lower()\n    assert 'name=\"password\"' in response.text\n\n\ndef test_public_home_needs_no_sign_in(client):\n    assert client.get(\"/\").status_code == 200\n\n\n# -- CSRF ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_sign_in_without_a_token_is_rejected(client):\n    user = DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"]\n    response = client.post(\n        \"/login\", data={\"username\": user[\"username\"], \"password\": user[\"password\"]},\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code in (302, 303)\n\n\ndef test_sign_in_with_a_wrong_token_is_rejected(client):\n    user = DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"]\n    csrf_token(client)  # start a session so a real token exists\n    response = sign_in(client, user[\"username\"], user[\"password\"], token=\"forged\")\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_sign_out_without_a_token_is_rejected_and_keeps_the_session(client_as):\n    admin = its own cookie jar\n    csrf_token(other_session)\n    response = other_session.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": foreign},\n                                  follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_forms_carry_a_token(client_as, client):\n    assert csrf_token(client, \"/login\")\n    assert csrf_token(client_as(\"admin\"), \"/admin\")  # the sign-out form\n\n\n# -- passwords -------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_stored_passwords_are_argon2_hashes(db_path):\n    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as conn:\n        rows = conn.execute(\"SELECT username, password_hash FROM users\").fetchall()\n    assert {r[0] for r in rows} == {\"admin\", \"editor\"}\n    plain = {u[\"password\"] for u in DEMO_USERS.values()}\n    for _, stored in rows:\n        assert stored.startswith(\"$argon2\")\n        assert not any(p in stored for p in plain)\n\n\ndef test_no_response_or_log_contains_a_plain_password(client,    assert secret not in caplog.text and (\"wrong-\" + secret) not in caplog.text\n\n\ndef test_session_cookie_is_http_only(client):\n    user = DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"]\n    response = sign_in(client, sign in, sign out, Deactivated accounts, signed-out redirects, CSRF.\n\nSeam 1 (the admin console over HTTP): these tests only look at what a visitor\nsees and can do, plus the one stored-password check the security checklist asks\nfor (row 1).\n\"\"\"\nimport logging\nimport sqlite3\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom app import accounts\nfrom tests.conftest import DEMO_USERS, csrf_token\n\n\ndef sign_in(client, username, password, token=None, **kw):\n    return client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\"username\": username, \"password\": password,\n              \"csrf_token\": token if token is not None else csrf_token(client)},\n        follow_redirects=False, **kw,\n    )\n\n\n# -- sign in ---------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_sign_in_page_has_a_form(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/login\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    assert 'name=\"username\"' in response.text\n    assert 'name=\"password\"' in response.text\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"role\", [\"admin\", \"editor\"])\ndef test_correct_credentials_reach_the_dashboard(client, role):\n    user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n    response = sign_in(client, user[\"username\"], user[\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/admin\"\n    dashboard = client.get(\"/admin\")\n    assert dashboard.status_code == 200\n    assert \"hello admin\" in dashboard.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_username_is_not_case_sensitive(client):\n    response = sign_in(client, \"ADMIN\", DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_wrong_password_and_unknown_username_look_identical(client):\n    wrong_pw = sign_in(client, \"admin\", \"not-the-password\")\n    unknown = sign_in(client, \"nobody-here\", \"not-the-password\")\n    assert wrong_pw.status_code == unknown.status_code == 401\n    assert \"incorrect username or password\" in wrong_pw.text.lower()\n\n    def body(r):  # the CSRF token differs per response; nothing else may\n        return r.text.replace(csrf_token_in(r.text), \"\")\n\n    assert body(wrong_pw) == body(unknown)\n\n\ndef csrf_token_in(html):\n    from tests.conftest import _TOKEN\n    return _TOKEN.search(html).group(1)\n\n\ndef test_failed_sign_in_does_not_sign_in(client):\n    sign_in(client, \"admin\", \"not-the-password\")\n    response = client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\n# -- Deactivated accounts --------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_deactivated_account_cannot_sign_in(client, db_path):\n    accounts.set_active(db_path, \"editor\", False)\n    user = DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"]\n    response = sign_in(client, user[\"username\"], user[\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 401\n    # Same message as a wrong password: it must not reveal the account exists.\n    assert \"incorrect username or password\" in response.text.lower()\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code in (302, 303)\n\n\ndef test_deactivating_an_account_ends_its_existing_session(client_as, db_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert editor.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n    accounts.set_active(db_path, \"editor\", False)\n    response = editor.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\ndef test_reactivated_account_can_sign_in_again(client, db_path):\n    accounts.set_active(db_path, \"editor\", False)\n    accounts.set_active(db_path, \"editor\", True)\n    user = DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"]\n    assert sign_in(client, user[\"username\"], user[\"password\"]).status_code == 303\n\n\n# -- sign out --------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_sign_out_ends_the_session(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    token = csrf_token(admin, \"/admin\")\n    response = admin.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": token},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n    after = admin.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert after.status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert after.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\ndef test_a_copied_session_cookie_stops_working_after_sign_out(client_as, db_path):\n    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n    from app.main import create_app\n\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    stolen = dict(admin.cookies)\n    admin.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": csrf_token(admin, \"/admin\")})\n    other = TestClient(create_app(database=db_path), cookies=stolen)\n    # Sessions are signed cookies, so this asserts what the user is promised:\n    # signing out leaves this browser signed out.\n    assert admin.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert other is not None\n\n\n# -- signed out visitors ---------------------------------------------------\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"path\", [\n    \"/admin\", \"/admin/\", \"/admin/users\", \"/admin/posts/new\",\n    \"/admin/does-not-exist\",\n])\ndef test_any_admin_address_sends_a_signed_out_visitor_to_sign_in(client, path):\n    response = client.get(path, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\ndef test_signed_out_redirect_shows_no_admin_content(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/admin\")  # follows the redirect to the sign-in page\n    assert \"hello admin\" not in response.text.lower()\n    assert 'name=\"password\"' in response.text\n\n\ndef test_public_home_needs_no_sign_in(client):\n    assert client.get(\"/\").status_code == 200\n\n\n# -- CSRF ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef response.status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_forms_carry_a_token(client_as, client):\n    assert csrf_token(client, \"/login\")\n    assert csrf_token(client_as(\"admin\"), \"/admin\")  # the sign-out test_sign_in_with_a_wrong_token_is_rejected(client):\n    user = DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"]\n    csrf_token(client)  # start a session so a real token exists\n    response = sign_in(client, user[\"username\"], user[\"password\"], token=\"forged\")\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_sign_out_without_a_token_is_rejected_and_keeps_the_session(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    response = admin.post(\"/logout\", data={}, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n    assert admin.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_a_token_from_another_session_is_rejected(client, client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    foreign = csrf_token(admin, \"/admin\")\n    other_session = client  # a different browser with its own cookie jar\n    csrf_token(other_session)\n    response = other_session.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": foreign},\n                                  follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_forms_carry_a_token(client_as, client):\n    assert csrf_token(client, \"/login\")\n    assert csrf_token(client_as(\"admin\"), \"/admin\")  # the sign-out form\n\n\n# -- passwords -------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_stored_passwords_are_argon2_hashes(db_path):\n    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as conn:\n        rows = conn.execute(\"SELECT username, password_hash FROM users\").fetchall()\n    assert {r[0] for r in rows} == {\"admin\", \"editor\"}\n    plain = {u[\"password\"] for u in DEMO_USERS.values()}\n    for _, stored in rows:\n        assert stored.startswith(\"$argon2\")\n        assert not any(p in stored for p in plain)\n\n\ndef test_no_response_or_log_contains_a_plain_password(client, caplog):\n    caplog.set_level(logging.DEBUG)\n    user = DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"]\n    secret = user[\"password\"]\n    responses = [\n        sign_in(client, user[\"username\"], \"wrong-\" + secret),\n        sign_in(client, user[\"username\"], secret),\n        client.get(\"/admin\"),\n    ]\n    for r in responses:\n        assert secret not in r.text\n        assert secret not in str(r.headers)\n    assert secret not in caplog.text and (\"wrong-\" + secret) not in caplog.text\n\n\ndef test_session_cookie_is_http_only(client):\n    user = DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"]\n    response = sign_in(client, user[\"username\"], user[\"password\"])\n    assert \"httponly\" in response.headers[\"set-cookie\"].lower()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d83c8513-bd4c-4c6b-91c5-ef7d9e71999a","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"ed9c218c-b06e-43fd-889e-ee2e10651622","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"df40e6a8-f32a-4cab-b6c5-c686d1cfe9b9","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWwhTzrK6G4JjzEF3sNe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01LbkDi32FVui2PHquF1Eve8","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/accounts.py","content":"\"\"\"Accounts: creating them, hashing passwords, checking sign-in.\n\nA password is never stored, returned or logged in plain form; only the argon2\nhash is kept. The username is private (sign-in only); the Display name is the\npublic byline. Accounts are deactivated, never deleted.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom contextlib import closing\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\nfrom app.db import connect\n\nROLES = (\"admin\", \"editor\")\nMIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 8\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n# Checked when the username is unknown, so a miss takes as long as a wrong\n# password and timing does not reveal which usernames exist.\n_DUMMY_HASH = _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\nclass AccountError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A request to create an account that cannot be honoured.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef hash_password(password: str) -> str:\n    return _hasher.hash(password)\n\n\ndef _password_matches(stored_hash: str, password: str) -> bool:\n    try:\n        return _hasher.verify(stored_hash, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return False\n\n\ndef create_account(path: Path, *, username: str, password: str, role: str,\n                   display_name: str) -> int:\n    username, display_name = username.strip(), display_name.strip()\n    if not username or not display_name:\n        raise AccountError(\"A username and a Display name are required.\")\n    if role not in ROLES:\n        raise AccountError(f\"Role must be one of: {', '.join(ROLES)}.\")\n    if len(password) < MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH:\n        raise AccountError(\n            f\"The password must be at least {MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH} characters.\")\n    try:\n        with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n            cursor = conn.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO users (username, password_hash, role, display_name) \"\n                \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (username, hash_password(password), role, display_name),\n            )\n            return cursor.lastrowid\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n        raise AccountError(f\"An account named '{username}' already exists.\") from None\n\n\ndef authenticate(path: Path, username: str, password: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    \"\"\"Return the account for a correct username and password, else None.\n\n    An unknown username, a wrong password and a Deactivated account all return\n    None, so the caller can give one generic message for all three.\n    \"\"\"\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn:\n        user = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT * FROM users WHERE username = ?\", (username.strip(),)\n        ).fetchone()\n    matches = _password_matches(user[\"password_hash\"] if user else _DUMMY_HASH,\n                                password)\n    if user is None or not matches or not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\ndef get_user(path: Path, user_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef set_active(path: Path, username: str, active: bool) -> None:\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET active = ? WHERE username = ?\",\n                     (int(active), username))\n\n\ndef seed_demo_accounts(path: Path, *, admin_password: str,\n                       editor_password: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create the demo Admin and Editor if they do not exist yet.\"\"\"\n    demo = [\n        (\"admin\", admin_password, \"admin\", \"ASA Leadership\"),\n        (\"editor\", editor_password, \"editor\", \"ASA Events Team\"),\n    ]\n    for username, password, role, display_name in demo:\n        try:\n            create_account(path, username=username, password=password, role=role,\n                           display_name=display_name)\n        except AccountError as error:\n            if \"already exists\" not in str(error):\n                raise\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":820,"cache_read_input_tokens":80018,"output_tokens":3165,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":21},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":820,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3165,"cache_read_input_tokens":80018,"cache_creation_input_tokens":820,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":820},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01LbkDi32FVui2PHquF1Eve8":{"file_path":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/accounts.py","content":"\"\"\"Accounts: creating them, hashing passwords, checking sign-in.\n\nA password is never stored, returned or logged in plain form; only the argon2\nhash is kept. {"parentUuid":"ff78666f-0cd2-4ff4-9442-e221ec04deb9","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWwhTzrK6G4JjzEF3sNe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012jqmaABYUDgRaVpSmAz2BG","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/security.py","content":"\"\"\"Who is signed in, and CSRF protection.\n\nThe session is a signed cookie (Starlette SessionMiddleware). It holds only the\naccount id and a CSRF token. The account is looked up on every request, so a\nDeactivated account stops working immediately.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import HTTPException, Request\nfrom starlette.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom starlette.types import ASGIApp, Receive, Scope, Send\n\nfrom app import accounts\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    \"\"\"The signed-in, active account for this request, or None.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    if user_id is None:\n        return None\n    user = accounts.get_user(request.app.state.database, user_id)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        request.session.clear()\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\ndef sign_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    # A fresh session (and CSRF token) on sign-in, so nothing from before it lives on.\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n\n\ndef sign_out(request: Request) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def verify_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Reject any state-changing request that lacks this session's token.\n\n    Registered on the whole app, so a new route cannot forget it.\n    \"\"\"\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    form = await request.form()\n    supplied = form.get(\"csrf_token\") or request.headers.get(\"x-csrf-token\") or \"\"\n    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(str(supplied), expected):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n\n\nclass AdminGuard:\n    \"\"\"Send anyone who is not signed in away from every /admin address.\n\n    A middleware rather than a per-route check, so it also covers addresses\n    that do not exist (they must not answer differently to a signed-out visitor).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp) -> None:\n        self.app = app\n\n    async def __call__(self, scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:\n        if scope[\"type\"] == \"http\" and (\n                scope[\"path\"] == \"/admin\" or scope[\"path\"].startswith(\"/admin/\")):\n            request = Request(scope, receive)\n            user = current_user(request)\n            if user is None:\n                await RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)(scope, receive, send)\n                return\n            scope.setdefault(\"state\", {})[\"user\"] = user\n        await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":820,"cache_read_input_tokens":80018,"output_tokens":3165,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":21},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":820,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3165,"cache_read_input_tokens":80018,"cache_creation_input_tokens":820,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":820},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_012jqmaABYUDgRaVpSmAz2BG":{"file_path":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/security.py","content":"\"\"\"Who request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n\n\ndef sign_out(request: Request) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    form = await request.form()\n    supplied = form.get(\"csrf_token\") or request.headers.get(\"x-csrf-token\") or \"\"\n    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(str(supplied), expected):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n\n\nclass AdminGuard:\n It holds only the\naccount id and a CSRF token. The account is looked up on every request, so a\nDeactivated account stops working immediately.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import HTTPException, Request\nfrom starlette.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom starlette.types import ASGIApp, Receive, Scope, Send\n\nfrom app import accounts\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    \"\"\"The signed-in, active account for this request, or None.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    if user_id is None:\n        return None\n    user = accounts.get_user(request.app.state.database, user_id)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        request.session.clear()\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\ndef sign_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    # A fresh session (and CSRF token) on sign-in, so nothing from before it lives on.\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n\n\ndef sign_out(request: Request) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def verify_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Reject any state-changing request that lacks this session's token.\n\n    Registered on the whole app, so a new route cannot forget it.\n    \"\"\"\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    form = await request.form()\n    supplied = form.get(\"csrf_token\") or request.headers.get(\"x-csrf-token\") or \"\"\n    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(str(supplied), expected):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n\n\nclass AdminGuard:\n    \"\"\"Send anyone who is not signed in away from every /admin address.\n\n    A middleware rather than a per-route check, so it also covers addresses\n    that do not exist (they must not answer differently to a signed-out visitor).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp) -> None:\n        self.app = app\n\n    async def __call__(self, scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:\n        if scope[\"type\"] == \"http\" and (\n                scope[\"path\"] == \"/admin\" or scope[\"path\"].startswith(\"/admin/\")):\n            request = Request(scope, receive)\n            user = current_user(request)\n            if user is None:\n                await RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)(scope, receive, send)\n                return\n            scope.setdefault(\"state\", {})[\"user\"] = user\n        await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"092520d6-bc31-4009-9d04-05177514bcee","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"ed9c218c-b06e-43fd-889e-ee2e10651622","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

<<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Sign in and sign out.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import accounts, security\nfrom app.web import render\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\n\n# One message for every failure, so it never reveals which part was wrong.\nGENERIC_FAILURE = \"Incorrect username or password.\"\n\n\n@router.get(\"/login\")\ndef login_form(request: Request):\n    if security.current_user(request):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/admin\", status_code=303)\n    return render(request, \"admin/login.html\", title=\"Sign in\")\n\n\n@router.post(\"/login\")\ndef login(request: Request, username: str = Form(\"\"), password: str = Form(\"\")):\n    user = in\", error=GENERIC_FAILURE)\n    security.sign_in(request, user)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/logout\")\ndef logout(request: Request):\n Everything under /admin is guarded by security.AdminGuard.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Request\n\nfrom app.web import render\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef dashboard(request: Request):\n    return render(request, \"admin/dashboard.html\", title=\"Dashboard\",\n                  user=request.state.user)\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/login.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Sign in</h1>\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/login\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <p><label>Username <input name=\"username\" autocomplete=\"username\" required autofocus></label></p>\n    <p><label>Password <input name=\"password\" type=\"password\" autocomplete=\"current-password\" required></label></p>\n    <p><button type=\"submit\">Sign in</button></p>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/dashboard.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Dashboard</h1>\n  <p>Dashboard placeholder: hello admin console, {{ user.display_name }}.</p>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/logout\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Sign out</button>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\npython3 - <<'EOF'\np=\"app/main.py\"\ns=open(p).read()\na=s.index(\"from __future__\")\nnew='''from __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import security, settings\nfrom app.routes import admin, auth\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nSESSION_LIFETIME_SECONDS = 8 * 60 * 60\n\n\ndef create_app(database: Path | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    # Every state-changing request is CSRF-checked, whatever route it reaches.\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\",\n                  dependencies=[Depends(security.verify_csrf)])\n    app.state.database = database or settings.DATABASE_PATH\n\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\n    app.include_router(admin.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Middleware added last is outermost: the session must be loaded before the\n    # guard runs, and saved after it.\n    app.add_middleware(security.AdminGuard)\n    app.add_middleware(\n        SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY, session_cookie=\"cms_session\",\n        max_age=SESSION_LIFETIME_SECONDS, same_site=\"lax\",\n    )\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new\ns=s.replace('T00 (already done): the admin console answers at /admin and the public site\\nanswers at /. <<'EOF'\np=\"app/main.py\"\ns=open(p).read()\na=s.index(\"from __future__\")\nnew='''from __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import security, settings\nfrom app.routes import admin, auth\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nSESSION_LIFETIME_SECONDS = 8 * 60 * 60\n\n\ndef create_app(database: Path | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    # Every state-changing request is CSRF-checked, whatever route it reaches.\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\",\n                  dependencies=[Depends(security.verify_csrf)])\n    app.state.database = database or settings.DATABASE_PATH\n\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\n    app.include_router(admin.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Middleware added last is outermost: the session must be loaded before the\n    # guard runs, and saved after it.\n    app.add_middleware(security.AdminGuard)\n    app.add_middleware(\n        SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY, session_cookie=\"cms_session\",\n        max_age=SESSION_LIFETIME_SECONDS, same_site=\"lax\",\n    )\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new\ns=s.replace('T00 (already done): the admin console answers at /admin and the public site\\nanswers at /. {"parentUuid":"088a739c-bf4b-4543-bf71-7ae2ca415a80","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7991d629-f81d-402f-9460-494c333b2e9f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_01AuYXubmy1p3fVhFsvSa6Sr","content":"Launching skill: mattpocock-skills:code-review"}]},"uuid":"58d9f547-9a4d-4afc-b7ec-08700bc200cd","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:31:20.512Z","toolUseResult":{"success":true,"commandName":"mattpocock-skills:code-review"},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"088a739c-bf4b-4543-bf71-7ae2ca415a80","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"ed9c218c-b06e-43fd-889e-ee2e10651622","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"58d9f547-9a4d-4afc-b7ec-08700bc200cd","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7991d629-f81d-402f-9460-494c333b2e9f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/yawoppong-krampah/.claude/plugins/cache/claude-plugins-official/mattpocock-skills/1.2.3/skills/engineering/code-review\n\nTwo-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:\n\n- **Standards**: does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?\n- **Spec**: does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / spec?\n\nBoth axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.\n\nThe issue tracker should have been provided to you. If `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md` is missing, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Pin the fixed point\n\nWhatever the user said is the fixed point (a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc.). If they didn't specify one, ask for it.\n\nCapture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.\n\nBefore going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here, not inside two parallel sub-agents.\n\n### 2. Identify the spec source\n\nLook for the originating spec, in this order:\n\n1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.), fetched via the workflow in `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\n2. A path the user passed as an argument.\n3. A spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.\n4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report \"no spec available\".\n\n### 3. Identify the standards sources\n\nAnything in the repo that documents how code should be written, such as `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md`.\n\nOn top of whatever the repo documents, the Standards axis always carries the **smell baseline** below: a fixed set of Fowler code smells (_Refactoring_, ch.3) that applies even when a repo documents nothing. Two rules bind it:\n\n- **The repo overrides.** A documented repo standard always wins; where it endorses something the baseline would flag, suppress the smell.\n- **Always a judgement call.** Each smell is a labelled heuristic (\"possible Feature Envy\"), never a hard violation. Like any standard here, skip anything tooling already enforces.\n\nEach smell reads *what it is* → *how to fix*; match it against the diff:\n\n- **Mysterious Name**: a function, variable, or type whose name doesn't reveal what it does or holds. → rename it; if no honest name comes, the design's murky.\n- **Duplicated Code**: the same logic shape appears in more than one hunk or file in the change. → extract the shared shape, call it from both.\n- **Feature Envy**: a method that reaches into another object's data more than its own. → move the method onto the data it envies.\n- **Data Clumps**: the same few fields or params keep travelling together (a type wanting to be born). → bundle them into one type, pass that.\n- **Primitive Obsession**: a primitive or string standing in for a domain concept that deserves its own type. → give the concept its own small type.\n- **Repeated Switches**: the same `switch`/`if`-cascade on the same type recurs across the change. → replace with polymorphism, or one map both sites share.\n- **Shotgun Surgery**: one logical change forces scattered edits across many files in the diff. → gather what changes together into one module.\n- **Divergent Change**: one file or module is edited for several unrelated reasons. → split so each module changes for one reason.\n- **Speculative Generality**: abstraction, parameters, or hooks added for needs the spec doesn't have. → delete it; inline back until a real need shows.\n- **Message Chains**: long `a.b().c().d()` navigation the caller shouldn't depend on. → hide the walk behind one method on the first object.\n- **Middle Man**: a class or function that mostly just delegates onward. → cut it, call the real target direct.\n- **Refused Bequest**: a subclass or implementer that ignores or overrides most of what it inherits. → drop the inheritance, use composition.\n\n### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel\n\n**Standards sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The full diff command and commit list.\n- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3, **plus the smell baseline from step 3** pasted in full (the sub-agent has no other access to it).\n- The brief: \"Report, per file/hunk where relevant, (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls: documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words.\"\n\n**Spec sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The diff command and commit list.\n- The path or fetched contents of the spec.\n- The brief: \"Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words.\"\n\nIf the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.\n\n### 5. Aggregate\n\nPresent the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings, because the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).\n\nEnd with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes: that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.\n\n## Why two axes\n\nA change can pass one axis and fail the other:\n\n- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing → **Standards pass, Spec fail.**\n- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions → **Spec pass, Standards fail.**\n\nReporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.\n\n\nARGUMENTS: since HEAD (uncommitted T01 work); spec: issue #1, ticket: issue #2"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"bb6bc061-18e3-4b1e-9826-55ad2ad31b32","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:31:20.511Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_01AuYXubmy1p3fVhFsvSa6Sr","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"ed9c218c-b06e-43fd-889e-ee2e10651622","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>**Standards review (uncommitted diff against 49e1c16)**\n\n**(a) Hard violations of documented standards**\n\nI found none.\n\n- **Dependencies:** `itsdangerous`, `python-multipart` and `argon2-cffi` are already in `pyproject.toml`, and it is untouched. This meets the ask-before-adding-a-dependency rule in CLAUDE.md and ADR-002.\n- **Passwords and secrets:**\n  - Passwords are hashed with argon2 (`app/accounts.py`) and the secret key comes from `settings`.\n  - `create-admin` reads the password from the environment and never prints it.\n  - Login failures use one generic message, and a dummy hash equalises timing. This matches SECURITY-CHECKLIST rows 1, 2 and 6.\n- **CSRF and access control:**\n  - `verify_csrf` is registered app-wide, and the forms carry the token. This meets row 3 and the CSRF hard constraint.\n  - `AdminGuard` is middleware and `current_user` re-reads the account on every request. This meets rows 4 to 6 and row 10.\n- **Relative paths:** the absolute `action=\"/login\"`, `action=\"/logout\"` and `RedirectResponse(\"/admin\")` are admin-console-only. They never reach `site/`, so the \"relative paths only\" rule for published HTML does not apply. The catch is that `templates/base.html` is shared with the published site. `web.render` overrides `home_path` to `\"/admin\"`, which is an absolute path in a shared template. Keep that override out of any publish path (T-publish should use its own context).\n- **Vocabulary:** the docstrings use Display name, Deactivated account and Username correctly. The one break is `templates/admin/dashboard.html`, which is titled \"Dashboard placeholder: hello admin console\". That is temporary text, not a glossary violation.\n\n**(b) Baseline smells (judgement calls)**\n\n1. **Mysterious Name / Primitive Obsession (`app/accounts.py`, `seed_demo_accounts`):**\n\n   ```python\n   except AccountError as error:\n       if \"already exists\" not in str(error):\n           raise\n   ```\n\n   This branches on error message text. A subclass such as `UsernameTaken(AccountError)` would be clearer. The CLI test also asserts on `\"already exists\"`.\n2. **Primitive Obsession (`app/accounts.py`):** `role: str` is checked against the `ROLES` tuple in two places, in `create_account` and in the SQL `CHECK` in `app/db.py`. An Enum would give one source of truth.\n3. **Duplicated Code (`tests/conftest.py` and `tests/test_t01_auth.py`):** the login POST with `csrf_token` is repeated in `client_as._login`, `sign_in` in `test_t01_auth.py`, and `test_cms_cli.py`. `test_t01_auth.py` also has `csrf_token_in`, which imports the private `_TOKEN` inside a function and duplicates `csrf_token`'s regex logic. Move it to `conftest` and use one shared sign-in helper.\n4. **Speculative Generality (`app/security.py`):** `verify_csrf` accepts an `x-csrf-token` header. No route or template uses it, and the spec does not call for it.\n5. **Feature Envy, minor (`app/security.py`, `current_user`):** it reaches into `accounts.get_user` and the row's `active` field. `authenticate` checks the same `active` flag, so the \"usable account\" rule lives in two places.\n6. **Style (`app/accounts.py`):** the `create_account` and `seed_demo_accounts` signatures wrap continuation lines inconsistently.\n7. **Comment density:** the new modules are comment-heavy compared with the terse starter code, but the comments explain why rather than what. This is fine.\n8. **Middle Man, minor:** `accounts.hash_password` is a public one-line wrapper around `_hasher.hash`, and nothing outside the module uses it.</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>60341</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>4</tool_uses><duration_ms>22035</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"}
