{"type":"queue-operation","operation":"enqueue","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:32:07.193Z","sessionId":"ed9c218c-b06e-43fd-889e-ee2e10651622","content":"<task-notification>\n<task-id>ab0a74de06ee0e321</task-id>\n<tool-use-id>toolu_01EgcmMwB2xCNo1yBn3o3rC4</tool-use-id>\n<output-file>/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab/tasks/ab0a74de06ee0e321.output</output-file>\n<status>completed</status>\n<summary>Agent \"Spec review of T01 diff\" finished</summary>\n<note>A task-notification fires each time this agent stops with no live background children of its own. The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>**T01 spec review.** `uv run pytest -q` passes (50 tests). Every acceptance criterion is at least partly met. The findings are below.\n\n**(a) Missing or partial**\n- **\"The existing T00 tests still pass.\"** The tests were not left as written. `test_admin_console_answers` was changed from `client` to `client_as(\"admin\")`. The original, signed out, expected 200 at `/admin`, and that now conflicts with \"Any admin address, visited while signed out, sends the visitor to sign-in.\" The edit is defensible, but it is a rewrite, not a pass. The dashboard placeholder also keeps the text \"hello admin\" only to satisfy that assertion. Say in the ticket comment that the T00 criterion was met by amendment.\n- **\"The password ... is never printed, logged or committed.\"** `.env.example` ships `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD=change-me-admin`, which passes the 8-character minimum. `create-admin` will create a real Admin with that well-known password if `.env` is copied unchanged. Nothing rejects it.\n- **\"`.env.example` lists every variable needed.\"** `CMS_SECRET_KEY` is listed, but `settings.py` falls back to `\"dev-only-not-for-production\"` when it is unset. Session cookies are then signed with a public key. Nothing warns or fails.\n\n**(b) Not asked for (scope creep, all minor)**\n- Minimum password length of 8, enforced in `create_account`. The spec sets no password policy.\n- Case-insensitive usernames (`COLLATE NOCASE`) and username trimming. These add behaviour and a test.\n- An 8-hour session lifetime.\n- `GET /login` redirects a signed-in user to `/admin`.\n- A dummy-hash timing equaliser in `accounts.py`. This is defensible under user story 4, but it wasn't requested.\n- The `x-csrf-token` header as a second CSRF channel.\n- The `--env-file` README and `.env.example` rewrites.\n- `accounts.set_active` has no caller outside the tests. It is arguably needed to test Deactivated accounts before T-user-management exists, so it is borderline.\n\n**(c) Implemented but possibly wrong**\n- **\"any existing session for it stops working.\"** This works, because `current_user` re-reads the account on every request. However, `AdminGuard` only checks paths under `/admin`. `/login` calls `security.current_user` itself, so that path is fine too. No gap found.\n- **\"a request without a valid token is rejected.\"** `verify_csrf` is an app-wide dependency and returns a bare JSON 403 (`Invalid or missing CSRF token.`). There is no rendered page. This meets the criterion but is rough for a user. Because it is a dependency and not middleware, it does not run on 404 paths. A POST to a nonexistent path returns 404, not 403. This is harmless.\n- **Fixture reconciliation** is correct and documented. Fixture passwords are fixed test values, not read from the environment. The ticket asks that seed passwords come from the environment, and only `seed_demo.py` does that. Test values are fine.\n- **\"the same demo accounts ... available to tests.\"** Tests build them with `seed_demo_accounts`, the same function the seed uses, so they stay in sync.\n\n**Checklist**\n\n| Criterion | Status |\n|---|---|\n| `cms create-admin`, password from environment | Met (see the `.env.example` default above) |\n| Demo seed with an Admin and an Editor, both with a Display name, and the `client_as` fixtures | Met |\n| Sign-in, generic failure message | Met (test compares wrong-password and unknown-username bodies) |\n| argon2, no plain password | Met |\n| Deactivated account and its existing session | Met |\n| Sign-out | Met |\n| Signed-out redirect | Met |\n| CSRF | Met |\n| `.env.example`, gitignore | Met (`.env` and `*.db` are ignored; no `.env` or `.db` is tracked) |\n| Tests | Met |\n| T00 tests | Passing, but one was edited (see (a)) |</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>66446</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>6</tool_uses><duration_ms>32525</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"}

The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>**T01 spec review.** `uv run pytest -q` passes (50 tests). Every acceptance criterion is at least partly met. The findings are below.\n\n**(a) Missing or partial**\n- **\"The existing T00 tests still pass.\"** The tests were not left as written. `test_admin_console_answers` was changed from `client` to `client_as(\"admin\")`. The original, signed out, expected 200 at `/admin`, and that now conflicts with \"Any admin address, visited while signed out, sends the visitor to sign-in.\" The edit is defensible, but it is a rewrite, not a pass. The dashboard placeholder also keeps the text \"hello admin\" only to satisfy that assertion. Say in the ticket comment that the T00 criterion was met by amendment.\n- **\"The password ... is never printed, logged or committed.\"** `.env.example` ships `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD=change-me-admin`, which passes the 8-character minimum. `create-admin` will create a real Admin with that well-known password if `.env` is copied unchanged. Nothing rejects it.\n- **\"`.env.example` lists every variable needed.\"** `CMS_SECRET_KEY` is listed, but `settings.py` falls back to `\"dev-only-not-for-production\"` when it is unset. Session cookies are then signed with a public key. Nothing warns or fails.\n\n**(b) Not asked for (scope creep, all minor)**\n- Minimum password length of 8, enforced in `create_account`. The spec sets no password policy.\n- Case-insensitive usernames (`COLLATE NOCASE`) and username trimming. These add behaviour and a test.\n- An 8-hour session lifetime.\n- `GET /login` redirects a signed-in user to `/admin`.\n- A dummy-hash timing equaliser in `accounts.py`. This is defensible under user story 4, but it wasn't requested.\n- The `x-csrf-token` header as a second CSRF channel.\n- The `--env-file` README and `.env.example` rewrites.\n- `accounts.set_active` has no caller outside the tests. It is arguably needed to test Deactivated accounts before T-user-management exists, so it is borderline.\n\n**(c) Implemented but possibly wrong**\n- **\"any existing session for it stops working.\"** This works, because `current_user` re-reads the account on every request. However, `AdminGuard` only checks paths under `/admin`. `/login` calls `security.current_user` itself, so that path is fine too. No gap found.\n- **\"a request without a valid token is rejected.\"** `verify_csrf` is an app-wide dependency and returns a bare JSON 403 (`Invalid or missing CSRF token.`). There is no rendered page. This meets the criterion but is rough for a user. Because it is a dependency and not middleware, it does not run on 404 paths. A POST to a nonexistent path returns 404, not 403. This is harmless.\n- **Fixture reconciliation** is correct and documented. Fixture passwords are fixed test values, not read from the environment. The ticket asks that seed passwords come from the environment, and only `seed_demo.py` does that. Test values are fine.\n- **\"the same demo accounts ... available to tests.\"** Tests build them with `seed_demo_accounts`, the same function the seed uses, so they stay in sync.\n\n**Checklist**\n\n| Criterion | Status |\n|---|---|\n| `cms create-admin`, password from environment | Met (see the `.env.example` default above) |\n| Demo seed with an Admin and an Editor, both with a Display name, and the `client_as` fixtures | Met |\n| Sign-in, generic failure message | Met (test compares wrong-password and unknown-username bodies) |\n| argon2, no plain password | Met |\n| Deactivated account and its existing session | Met |\n| Sign-out | Met |\n| Signed-out redirect | Met |\n| CSRF | Met |\n| `.env.example`, gitignore | Met (`.env` and `*.db` are ignored; no `.env` or `.db` is tracked) |\n| Tests | Met |\n| T00 tests | Passing, but one was edited (see (a)) |</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>66446</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>6</tool_uses><duration_ms>32525</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"},"uuid":"5166035d-a8f0-4195-9ba6-9454276ba875","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:32:07.208Z","permissionMode":"bypassPermissions","origin":{"kind":"task-notification","producer":"session-task"},"promptSource":"system","turnOrigin":"task_notification","turnPosition":{"promptIndex":1,"turnIndex":3},"queueSkipAttachments":true,"userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"ed9c218c-b06e-43fd-889e-ee2e10651622","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"24678c1a-d2b6-4ff7-92a7-79cc925a690c","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWwtgs4wr7cNrGEr5P2C","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"T01 is committed as `0cf7d23` on `main` (not pushed). All 50 tests pass, and I also checked sign-in, redirect and CSRF with `curl` against a running server.\n\n**What now works**\n- **Sign-in:** sign in with a username and password. A wrong password, an unknown username and a Deactivated account all get the same message, \"Incorrect username or password.\"\n- **Deactivated accounts:** an existing session for a deactivated account stops working on its next request.\n- **Sign-out and redirects:** `/logout` ends the session, and any `/admin…` address (including ones that don't exist) redirects a signed-out visitor to `/login`.\n- **CSRF:** every state-changing request is checked app-wide, so a new route can't forget it.\n- **Passwords:** stored as argon2 hashes only.\n- **First Admin:** `cms create-admin --username NAME --display-name \"…\"` reads `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` from the environment and never prints it. `scripts/seed_demo.py` creates the demo `admin` and `editor` accounts.\n- **Fixtures:** `client_as(\"admin\")` and `client_as(\"editor\")` now sign in by username, using a temporary database seeded with those accounts.\n- **Docs:** the README and `.env.example` now say to load `.env` with `uv run --env-file .env`, because nothing reads it automatically.\n\n**Two things to know**\n- **I edited one T00 test.** `test_admin_console_answers` originally expected an anonymous 200 at `/admin`, which contradicts the sign-in redirect this ticket requires. It now signs in as admin first. So \"T00 tests still pass\" is met by amendment for that one test. The other three are untouched.\n- **Signed-cookie sessions are stateless.** A copied cookie stays valid after sign-out until it expires (8 hours) or the account is deactivated. That follows from \"session cookie signed by the server\" in the spec.\n\n**Code review**\n- **Fixed:** the review found no hard violations of the repo's standards. I fixed three small smells: matching on an error message, an unused CSRF header path, and duplicated test helpers.\n- **Left open:** `.env.example` ships `change-me-admin` as a password, and `CMS_SECRET_KEY` falls back to a public default when unset. I accepted both for a local-only console. Both are worth a check at T08.\n- **Posted:** findings and resolutions are on issue #2, including the unresolved items: https://github.com/yawoppongkrampah-hub/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/2#issuecomment-5882530574.\n\n`notes/usage-ledger.csv` and the session transcript were already uncommitted before this session, so I left them out of the commit. Say the word if you want me to push."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":523,"cache_read_input_tokens":106310,"output_tokens":918,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":523,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":918,"cache_read_input_tokens":106310,"cache_creation_input_tokens":523,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":523},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWwtgNYnuWp7JzL91Vbm","type":"assistant","uuid":"9c8b9176-16f3-4531-8e6a-680995443c0a","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:32:35.301Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"ed9c218c-b06e-43fd-889e-ee2e10651622","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"079c8fda-1c71-4b40-871d-f1dbcd467b90","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWx6ij1YujEh5PBhznZh","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_019P1cHzyRaTnGa67xhqvVdt","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"gh issue comment 9 --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Follow-up from T01 (#2, commit 0cf7d23) — review before the demo release\n\n1. **Public fallback `CMS_SECRET_KEY`.** `app/settings.py` falls back to `\"dev-only-not-for-production\"` when the variable is unset, so session cookies would be signed with a key that is in the repo. Decide whether the app should refuse to start (or warn loudly) without a real key.\n2. **Well-known demo passwords in `.env.example`.** `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD=change-me-admin` and `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD=change-me-editor` pass the 8-character minimum, so `cms create-admin` and `scripts/seed_demo.py` will create accounts with those passwords if `.env` is copied unchanged. Consider rejecting the placeholder values, and make the release checklist say to change them.\n3. **Known limitation to review: replayed session cookies.** Sessions are stateless signed cookies (as the spec says). A copied cookie stays valid after sign-out until it expires (8 hours) or the account is Deactivated. Decide whether to accept this for the class demo and document it, or add a server-side session record.\n\nRecorded in the T01 review comment: https://github.com/yawoppongkrampah-hub/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/2#issuecomment-5882530574\nEOF\n)\" | tail -1; echo; git status","description":"Comment follow-up on T08 and show git status"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":578,"cache_read_input_tokens":107940,"output_tokens":663,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":578,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":663,"cache_read_input_tokens":107940,"cache_creation_input_tokens":578,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":578},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_019P1cHzyRaTnGa67xhqvVdt":{"command":"cd /Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms && gh issue comment 9 --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Follow-up from T01 (#2, commit 0cf7d23) — review before the demo release\n\n1. **Public fallback `CMS_SECRET_KEY`.** `app/settings.py` falls back to `\"dev-only-not-for-production\"` when the variable is unset, so session cookies would be signed with a key that is in the repo. Decide whether the app should refuse to start (or warn loudly) without a real key.\n2. **Well-known demo passwords in `.env.example`.** `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD=change-me-admin` and `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD=change-me-editor` pass the 8-character minimum, so `cms create-admin` and `scripts/seed_demo.py` will create accounts with those passwords if `.env` is copied unchanged. Details are in the commit and in the review comment on #2 (https://github.com/yawoppongkrampah-hub/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/2#issuecomment-5882530574).\n- Follow-ups for the T08 release are recorded on #9 (https://github.com/yawoppongkrampah-hub/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/9#issuecomment-5882558716): fallback `CMS_SECRET_KEY`, placeholder demo passwords, replayed-cookie limitation.\n- Next ticket is probably **T02 (#3)**: an Editor writes a Post, with roles and Permission refusal. Read #3, spec #1 and `CONTEXT.md` first (see `CLAUDE.md`).\n- 50 tests pass with `uv run pytest -q`.\n- Uncommitted and deliberately left out of T01: `notes/usage-ledger.csv` (modified) and `docs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_06_your-name_20260928.md` (untracked). Ask the user before committing them.\n- `uv run python scripts/usage_report.py --remaining 7` says the remaining tickets FIT the cap. T01 cost about 3% of the 5h window.\n\n## Things not obvious from the code\n- Sign-in identifier is the **username**, not an email. `tests/conftest.py` `client_as(role)` signs in with a CSRF token against a per-test seeded temp DB. `csrf_token(client, path)` and `token_in(html)` are the helpers for tests that POST.\n- `test_t00.py::test_admin_console_answers` was edited on purpose to sign in first, because signed-out `/admin` now redirects to `/login`.\n- `create_app(database=Path)` takes a DB path (default `settings.DATABASE_PATH`). Schema is created on first `db.connect()`.\n- Guards: `security.AdminGuard` middleware protects every `/admin*` path. CSRF is an app-wide dependency (`security.verify_csrf`), so new POST routes get it automatically. Admin templates should render through `web.render` to get the token; forms need `<input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">`.\n- `web.render` sets `home_path=\"/admin\"` on the shared `base.html`. Never reuse that context in the publish step.\n- `.env` is not auto-loaded. Use `uv run --env-file .env ...`.\n- Admin pages have no stylesheet yet (`base.html` links a relative `style.css`, which 404s under the admin).\n- Session-per-phase rule: write the ticket id (e.g. `T02`) to `.claude/state/phase` at the start of the session. That file is gitignored. Commits must start `T0N:` and end with `Closes #N`, and code-review findings must be posted on the issue before the closing commit.\n- No non-Anthropic backend was in use (base URL unset), so no `Backend:` commit trailer is needed unless `/status` shows one.\n- Commit attribution trailer from the session: `Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.\n\n## Suggested skills\n- `mattpocock-skills:implement` with the ticket number (e.g. `#3`) to run the ticket end to end.\n- `mattpocock-skills:tdd` for the test-first loop at the HTTP seam.\n- `mattpocock-skills:code-review` before the closing commit.
